This certifies root-font enlargement, not browser zoom. Screenshot: `artifacts/review-170/narrow-double-text.png`.\\n\\nA targeted local network block on Activity assignment/response reads showed a friendly read error and Retry Activity review. Removing the block and clicking Retry returned the unavailable-in-this-account state for the QA student\'s assignment under the teacher\'s personal Review route. This is genuine read-failure/retry and owner-denial evidence, not a positive owned Activity miss. Screenshot: `artifacts/review-170/activity-read-failure.png`. All network blocks and viewport overrides were cleared.\\n\\n## Outstanding acceptance\\n\\nWhole #170 remains open. Positive ordinary-student Activity-miss detail, broader source task walks, review completion/flag independence in a real account, later-miss episodes, first/blind/reveal behavior across all supported families, and teacher/student queue agreement still require acceptance. This pass did not manufacture new misses or mutate student work to provide them. Full runtime detail coverage is not inferred from mocked tests.\\n\\nThe separate #176 backend deployment still requires its outstanding route confirmation and immediate credit approval for a paid Lovable agent turn. This frontend slice needs no backend change.\\n\\n## Published frontend checkpoint\\n\\nFunctional commit `cc294c7227281a5db4ee38c1ada595fd20ed2a02` reached GitHub main through `.claude/safe-push.sh`, including its typecheck gate. Lovable imported that exact SHA. Free deployment `592d5dad-5f50-4e93-9b89-aec7a6c80f9a` completed with the editor showing Your website was updated. The initial fresh production check served the previous UI; after the deployment transition, a reload rendered the new area/source controls.\\n\\nAuthenticated production `lsatprep.study/review/to-review` displayed the same owned journal row. \'\'}`} {...props} />;\\n}\\nfunction EpisodePanel({ ownerId, source, sourceId, requestedEpisodeId }: Props) {\\n  const [episodes, setEpisodes] = useState<ReviewEpisode[]>([]);\\n  const [events, setEvents] = useState<ReviewEpisodeEvent[]>([]);\\n  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);\\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true); const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null); const [reload, setReload] = useState(0);\\n  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState(\'\'); const [reflection, setReflection] = useState(\'\');\\n  const [errorDiagnosis, setErrorDiagnosis] = useState(\'\'); const [rightDiagnosis, setRightDiagnosis] = useState(\'\');\\n  const pending = useRef<{ episode: ReviewEpisode; id: string; kind: ReviewEventKind; payload: Record<string, string> } | null>(null);\\n  const mounted = useRef(true);\\n  useEffect(() => { mounted.current = true; return () => { mounted.current = false; }; }, []);\\n  useEffect(() => {\\n    let active = true; setLoading(true); setError(null);\\n    void service.list(ownerId, ownerId).then(result => {\\n      if (!active) return;\\n      const matching = result.episodes.filter(episode => episode.source_kind === source && episode.source_id === sourceId);\\n      setEpisodes(matching); setEvents(result.events);\\n      if (requestedEpisodeId && !matching.some(episode => episode.id === requestedEpisodeId)) {\\n        setSelected(null); setError(\'The requested review task is unavailable for this saved submission.\'); return;\\n      }\\n      const incomplete = matching.filter(episode => !reviewEpisodeState(result.events.filter(event => event.episode_id === episode.id)).completed);\\n      const preferred = incomplete.find(episode => episode.reason === \'teacher_selected\') ?? incomplete[0] ?? matching[0];\\n      setSelected(current => requestedEpisodeId ?? (matching.some(episode => episode.id === current) ? current : preferred?.id ?? null));\\n    }).catch(() => { if (active) setError(\'Saved review work could not be loaded. Try again before opening or completing a task.\'); })\\n      .finally(() => { if (active) setLoading(false); });\\n    return () => { active = false; };\\n  }, [ownerId, source, sourceId, requestedEpisodeId, reload]);\\n  const episode = episodes.find(item => item.id === selected) ?? null;\\n  const state = reviewEpisodeState(events.filter(event => event.episode_id === episode?.id));\\n  const completion = events.find(event => event.episode_id === episode?.id && event.kind === \'complete\');\\n  async function open() {\\n    setBusy(true); setError(null);\\n    try {\\n      const saved = await service.open({ actorId: ownerId, studentId: ownerId, source, sourceId, reason: \'missed\' });\\n      if (!mounted.current) return;\\n      setEpisodes(current => current.some(item => item.id === saved.id) ? current : [...current, saved]); setSelected(saved.id);\\n      // The idempotent open can recover an existing episode after a lost response.\\n      setReload(current => current + 1);\\n    } catch { if (mounted.current) setError(\'Review task was not confirmed in your account. Try opening it again.\'); }\\n    finally { if (mounted.current) setBusy(false); }\\n  }\\n  async function save(kind?: ReviewEventKind, payload?: Record<string, string>) {\\n    if (!episode) return;\\n    if (!pending.current && kind && payload) pending.current = { episode, id: crypto.randomUUID(), kind, payload };\\n    const request = pending.current; if (!request) return;\\n    setBusy(true); setError(null);\\n    try {\\n      const saved = await service.append(ownerId, request.episode, request.id, request.kind, request.payload);\\n      if (!mounted.current) return;\\n      setEvents(current => current.some(event => event.id === saved.id) ? current : [...current, saved]); pending.current = null;\\n    } catch { if (mounted.current) setError(\'Review work was not confirmed in your account. Your text is retained. Retry this save before continuing.\'); }\\n    finally { if (mounted.current) setBusy(false); }\\n  }\\n  const blocked = busy || pending.current !== null || error !== null;\\n  return <section aria-label=\\"Saved review task\\" className=\\"space-y-4 rounded-xl border p-5\\">\\n    <h2 className=\\"text-lg font-semibold\\">Save your review work</h2>\\n    <p className=\\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">This task records new review work for this exact saved submission. The original answer, outcome and flags stay unchanged. Source feedback may already have been visible; a new answer or Reveal here does not reconstruct historical Blind Review.</p>\\n    {loading ? <p role=\\"status\\">Loading saved review work…</p> : <>\\n      {error && <div role=\\"alert\\"><p>{error}</p>{pending.current ? <button disabled={busy} className=\\"min-h-11 underline\\" onClick={() => void save()}>Retry save</button> : <button disabled={busy} className=\\"min-h-11 underline\\" onClick={() => setReload(current => current + 1)}>Reload review work</button>}</div>}\\n      {!episode ? !error && <button disabled={busy} className=\\"min-h-11 underline\\" onClick={() => void open()}>Open review task</button> : <>\\n        {episodes.length > 1 && <label className=\\"block\\">Review task<select aria-label=\\"Review task\\" disabled={blocked} value={selected ?? \'\'} onChange={event => { setSelected(event.target.value); setAnswer(\'\'); setReflection(\'\'); setErrorDiagnosis(\'\'); setRightDiagnosis(\'\'); }}>{episodes.map(item => <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>{item.reason === \'teacher_selected\' ? \'Teacher selection\' : \'Missed submission\'}</option>)}</select></label>}\\n        {episode.selection_reason && <p>Teacher’s reason: {episode.selection_reason}</p>}\\n        {state.completed ? <>\\n          <p role=\\"status\\">This review task is saved as reviewed. Its source answer and flag are unchanged.</p>\\n          {state.blindAnswer !== null && <p>Saved review answer: {state.blindAnswer}</p>}\\n          {state.revealed && <p>Reveal recorded for this review task.</p>}\\n          {state.reflection !== null && <p>Saved notes: {state.reflection}</p>}\\n          {completion?.payload.error_diagnosis && <p>Saved error diagnosis: {completion.payload.error_diagnosis}</p>}\\n          {completion?.payload.right_answer_diagnosis && <p>Saved right-answer diagnosis: {completion.payload.right_answer_diagnosis}</p>}\\n        </> : <>\\n          {state.blindAnswer !== null ? <p>Saved review answer: {state.blindAnswer}</p> : !state.revealed && <div><label className=\\"block\\">Optional answer before recording Reveal<textarea aria-label=\\"Optional review answer\\" disabled={blocked} value={answer} onChange={event => setAnswer(event.target.value)} className=\\"block w-full rounded border p-2\\" /></label><button className=\\"min-h-11 underline\\" disabled={blocked || !answer.trim()} onClick={() => void save(\'blind_answer\', { answer })}>Save review answer</button></div>}\\n          {state.revealed ? <p>Reveal recorded for this review task.</p> : <button disabled={blocked} className=\\"min-h-11 underline\\" onClick={() => void save(\'reveal\', {})}>Record Reveal for this task</button>}\\n          <label className=\\"block\\">Review notes<textarea aria-label=\\"Review notes\\" disabled={blocked} value={reflection} onChange={event => setReflection(event.target.value)} className=\\"block w-full rounded border p-2\\" /></label>\\n          {state.reflection !== null && <p>Saved notes: {state.reflection}</p>}\\n          <button disabled={blocked || !reflection.trim()} className=\\"min-h-11 underline\\" onClick={() => void save(\'reflection\', { text: reflection })}>Save review notes</button>\\n          {episode.reason === \'missed\' && <>\\n            <label className=\\"block\\">What caused the error?<textarea aria-label=\\"Error diagnosis\\" disabled={blocked} value={errorDiagnosis} onChange={event => setErrorDiagnosis(event.target.value)} className=\\"block w-full rounded border p-2\\" /></label>\\n            <label className=\\"block\\">Why is the right answer right?<textarea aria-label=\\"Right answer diagnosis\\" disabled={blocked} value={rightDiagnosis} onChange={event => setRightDiagnosis(event.target.value)} className=\\"block w-full rounded border p-2\\" /></label>\\n          </>}\\n          <button disabled={blocked || episode.reason === \'missed\' && (!errorDiagnosis.trim() || !rightDiagnosis.trim())} className=\\"min-h-11 underline\\" onClick={() => void save(\'complete\', episode.reason === \'missed\' ? { error_diagnosis: errorDiagnosis, right_answer_diagnosis: rightDiagnosis } : {})}>Mark reviewed</button>\\n        </>}\\n      </>}\\n    </>}\\n  </section>;\\n}\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"49b180","wall_time_seconds":1.2538821,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":26,"output":"src\\\\bootcamps\\\\rc-bootcamp\\\\persistence.ts\\nsrc\\\\bootcamps\\\\main-conclusion\\\\modules\\\\diagnostics\\\\RrReview.tsx\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"122bc5","wall_time_seconds":0.5910038,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":2003,"output":"-- Unapplied additive contract. No source attempt, flag, journal or note writes.\\nBEGIN;\\nSET LOCAL lock_timeout = \'3s\';\\nSET LOCAL statement_timeout = \'45s\';\\nCREATE TABLE public.review_episodes_v1 (\\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\\n  student_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id),\\n  actor_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id),\\n  source_kind text NOT NULL CHECK (source_kind IN (\'intro_lr_attempts\',\'hs_capstone\')),\\n  source_id text NOT NULL, question_id text NOT NULL,\\n  reason text NOT NULL CHECK (reason IN (\'missed\',\'teacher_selected\')),\\n  selection_reason text,\\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT clock_timestamp(),\\n  CHECK (reason <> \'teacher_selected\' OR length(btrim(selection_reason)) > 0 AND selection_reason IS NOT NULL),\\n  UNIQUE(student_id, source_kind, source_id, reason, actor_id)\\n);\\nCREATE TABLE public.review_episode_events_v1 (\\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY, episode_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.review_episodes_v1(id),\\n  actor_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id),\\n  kind text NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN (\'blind_answer\',\'reveal\',\'reflection\',\'complete\')),\\n  payload jsonb NOT NULL CHECK (jsonb_typeof(payload) = \'object\'),\\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT clock_timestamp()\\n);\\nCREATE INDEX review_episode_student_v1 ON public.review_episodes_v1(student_id,created_at);\\nCREATE INDEX review_episode_event_v1 ON public.review_episode_events_v1(episode_id,created_at);\\nALTER TABLE public.review_episodes_v1 ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\nALTER TABLE public.review_episode_events_v1 ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\nCREATE POLICY review_episode_read_v1 ON public.review_episodes_v1 FOR SELECT TO authenticated\\nUSING (student_id = auth.uid() OR public.has_role(auth.uid(),\'admin\'));\\nCREATE POLICY review_event_read_v1 ON public.review_episode_events_v1 FOR SELECT TO authenticated\\nUSING (EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.review_episodes_v1 e WHERE e.id=episode_id AND (e.student_id=auth.uid() OR public.has_role(auth.uid(),\'admin\'))));\\nREVOKE ALL ON public.review_episodes_v1,public.review_episode_events_v1 FROM PUBLIC,anon,authenticated;\\nGRANT SELECT ON public.review_episodes_v1,public.review_episode_events_v1 TO authenticated;\\n\\nCREATE FUNCTION public.open_review_episode_v1(p_student uuid,p_source text,p_source_id text,p_reason text,p_selection_reason text DEFAULT NULL)\\nRETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path=pg_catalog,public AS $$\\nDECLARE actor uuid:=auth.uid(); q text; correct boolean; existing public.review_episodes_v1%ROWTYPE;\\nBEGIN\\n  IF actor IS NULL OR p_student IS NULL OR p_reason NOT IN (\'missed\',\'teacher_selected\') OR p_reason IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'Invalid review actor or reason\'; END IF;\\n  IF p_reason=\'teacher_selected\' THEN\\n    IF NOT public.has_role(actor,\'admin\') OR actor=p_student OR p_selection_reason IS NULL OR length(btrim(p_selection_reason))=0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'Teacher selection requires admin access and a reason\'; END IF;\\n  ELSIF actor<>p_student THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'Only the student may open a missed episode\'; END IF;\\n  IF p_source=\'intro_lr_attempts\' THEN\\n    SELECT a.question_id,a.correct INTO q,correct FROM public.intro_lr_attempts a WHERE a.user_id=p_student AND a.id::text=p_source_id FOR SHARE;\\n  ELSIF p_source=\'hs_capstone\' THEN\\n    SELECT k.data->>\'item_id\',(k.data->>\'correct\')::boolean INTO q,correct FROM public.bootcamp_kv k\\n    WHERE k.user_id=p_student AND k.table_name=\'calibration_attempts\' AND k.record_id=p_source_id\\n      AND k.data->>\'user_id\'=p_student::text AND k.data->>\'calibration_module\'=\'M5\' AND k.data->\'calibration_only\'=\'true\'::jsonb FOR SHARE;\\n  ELSE RAISE EXCEPTION \'Unsupported review source\'; END IF;\\n  IF q IS NULL OR correct IS NULL OR (p_reason=\'missed\' AND correct) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'Owned source does not support this review reason\'; END IF;\\n  INSERT INTO public.review_episodes_v1(student_id,actor_id,source_kind,source_id,question_id,reason,selection_reason)\\n    VALUES(p_student,actor,p_source,p_source_id,q,p_reason,CASE WHEN p_reason=\'teacher_selected\' THEN btrim(p_selection_reason) ELSE NULL END)\\n    ON CONFLICT(student_id,source_kind,source_id,reason,actor_id) DO NOTHING;\\n  SELECT * INTO existing FROM public.review_episodes_v1 WHERE student_id=p_student AND actor_id=actor AND source_kind=p_source AND source_id=p_source_id AND reason=p_reason;\\n  IF p_reason=\'teacher_selected\' AND existing.selection_reason<>btrim(p_selection_reason) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'Existing selection has a different reason\'; END IF;\\n  RETURN to_jsonb(existing);\\nEND $$;\\n\\nCREATE FUNCTION public.append_review_event_v1(p_episode uuid,p_event uuid,p_kind text,p_payload jsonb)\\nRETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path=pg_catalog,public AS $$\\nDECLARE actor uuid:=auth.uid(); episode public.review_episodes_v1%ROWTYPE; prior public.review_episode_events_v1%ROWTYPE; saved public.review_episode_events_v1%ROWTYPE;\\nBEGIN\\n  SELECT * INTO episode FROM public.review_episodes_v1 WHERE id=p_episode FOR UPDATE;\\n  IF actor IS NULL OR episode.id IS NULL OR actor<>episode.student_id THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'Only the student may record review work\'; END IF;\\n  SELECT * INTO prior FROM public.review_episode_events_v1 WHERE id=p_event;\\n  IF prior.id IS NOT NULL THEN\\n    IF prior.episode_id<>p_episode OR prior.actor_id<>actor OR prior.kind IS DISTINCT FROM p_kind OR prior.payload IS DISTINCT FROM p_payload THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'Review event identity conflict\'; END IF;\\n    RETURN to_jsonb(prior);\\n  END IF;\\n  IF p_event IS NULL OR p_kind IS NULL OR p_kind NOT IN (\'blind_answer\',\'reveal\',\'reflection\',\'complete\') OR p_payload IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p_payload)<>\'object\'\\n    OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_each(p_payload) v WHERE jsonb_typeof(v.value)<>\'string\') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'Invalid review event\'; END IF;\\n  IF EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.review_episode_events_v1 WHERE episode_id=p_episode AND kind=\'complete\') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'This review episode is complete\'; END IF;\\n  IF p_kind=\'blind_answer\' THEN\\n    IF coalesce(length(btrim(p_payload->>\'answer\')),0)=0 OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.review_episode_events_v1 WHERE episode_id=p_episode AND kind IN (\'blind_answer\',\'reveal\')) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'Blind answer must precede reveal and is immutable\'; END IF;\\n  END IF;\\n  IF p_kind=\'reveal\' AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.review_episode_events_v1 WHERE episode_id=p_episode AND kind=\'reveal\') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'Reveal already recorded\'; END IF;\\n  IF p_kind=\'complete\' AND episode.reason=\'missed\' AND (coalesce(length(btrim(p_payload->>\'error_diagnosis\')),0)=0 OR coalesce(length(btrim(p_payload->>\'right_answer_diagnosis\')),0)=0) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'Miss completion requires error and right-answer diagnoses\'; END IF;\\n  INSERT INTO public.review_episode_events_v1(id,episode_id,actor_id,kind,payload) VALUES(p_event,p_episode,actor,p_kind,p_payload) RETURNING * INTO saved;\\n  RETURN to_jsonb(saved);\\nEND $$;\\n\\nCREATE FUNCTION public.read_review_episodes_v1(p_student uuid)\\nRETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path=pg_catalog,public AS $$\\nBEGIN\\n  IF auth.uid() IS NULL OR p_student IS NULL OR (auth.uid()<>p_student AND NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(),\'admin\')) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'Review access denied\'; END IF;\\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object(\'episodes\',coalesce((SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(e) ORDER BY e.created_at,e.id) FROM public.review_episodes_v1 e WHERE e.student_id=p_student),\'[]\'::jsonb),\\n    \'events\',coalesce((SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(v) ORDER BY [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 12.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"299bfe","wall_time_seconds":0.9613613,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1400,"output":"/**\\n * Progress persistence for the RC bootcamp lesson.\\n *\\n * Two implementations behind one adapter. localStorage is the parity path with\\n * the approved prototype (same key, same shape, same guards) and is what a\\n * signed-out preview uses. For a signed-in student the Supabase adapter writes\\n * the same record to `rc_lesson_progress` AND to localStorage, so a dropped\\n * connection never costs the student their writing.\\n *\\n * Nothing here invents progress. A read that cannot be trusted returns an empty\\n * record and the lesson starts at the beginning; a remote write that fails\\n * reports failure rather than showing a \\"Saved\\" state that did not happen.\\n *\\n * `rc_lesson_progress` is not in the generated Supabase types yet (it ships in\\n * the same push as this file), so the client is cast at the call site — the\\n * established pattern in this repo for tables ahead of a types regeneration.\\n */\\n\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\nimport { TOTAL_SCREENS } from \'./content\';\\n\\n/** localStorage key — unchanged from the prototype so an in-progress local run survives the port. */\\nexport const STORE_KEY = \'rc-ewtp-v1\';\\n\\n/** Reserved keys inside the flat record. Everything else is the student\'s own work. */\\nexport const AT_KEY = \'__at\';\\nexport const FURTHEST_KEY = \'__furthest\';\\nexport const COMPLETED_KEY = \'__completedAt\';\\n/** Client-written timestamp used to reconcile this browser with the cloud. */\\nexport const SAVED_AT_KEY = \'__savedAt\';\\n\\nexport type LessonData = Record<string, string | number>;\\n\\nexport interface PersistenceAdapter {\\n  load(): Promise<LessonData>;\\n  save(data: LessonData): Promise<boolean>;\\n}\\n\\n/** Integer-guard a stored screen index. Anything else is discarded. */\\nexport function safeIndex(value: unknown): number | null {\\n  return Number.isInteger(value) && (value as number) >= 0 && (value as number) < TOTAL_SCREENS\\n    ? (value as number)\\n    : null;\\n}\\n\\nfunction isPlainRecord(value: unknown): value is LessonData {\\n  return !!value && typeof value === \'object\' && !Array.isArray(value);\\n}\\n\\nfunction savedAt(data: LessonData): number {\\n  const value = data[SAVED_AT_KEY];\\n  return typeof value === \'string\' ? Date.parse(value) || 0 : 0;\\n}\\n\\nfunction stamp(data: LessonData): LessonData {\\n  return { ...data, [SAVED_AT_KEY]: new Date().toISOString() };\\n}\\n\\n/** Prefer the newest complete snapshot. Legacy local records win so unsynced work is never discarded. */\\nexport function chooseLatestProgress(\\n  local: LessonData,\\n  remote: LessonData | null,\\n  remoteUpdatedAt?: string,\\n): LessonData {\\n  if (!remote) return local;\\n  if (Object.keys(local).length === 0) return remote;\\n  const localTime = savedAt(local);\\n  const remoteTime = savedAt(remote) || (remoteUpdatedAt ? Date.parse(remoteUpdatedAt) || 0 : 0);\\n  if (localTime === 0) return local;\\n  return remoteTime > localTime ? remote : local;\\n}\\n\\nexport const localAdapter: PersistenceAdapter = {\\n  async load() {\\n    try {\\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY);\\n      if (!raw) return {};\\n      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);\\n      return isPlainRecord(parsed) ? parsed : {};\\n    } catch {\\n      return {};\\n    }\\n  },\\n  async save(data) {\\n    try {\\n      localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(stamp(data)));\\n      return true;\\n    } catch {\\n      return false;\\n    }\\n  },\\n};\\n\\n/**\\n * Supabase-backed progress for a signed-in student, mirrored to localStorage.\\n * If the remote row is unavailable for any reason the local record is used, so\\n * the lesson degrades to prototype parity instead of losing the student\'s work.\\n */\\nexport function createSupabaseAdapter(userId: string, lessonId: string): PersistenceAdapter {\\n  // Keep cloud writes ordered. Without this, a slow older request can arrive\\n  // after a newer one and move a student backwards.\\n  let cloudQueue: Promise<boolean> = Promise.resolve(true);\\n\\n  async function writeCloud(data: LessonData): Promise<boolean> {\\n    try {\\n      const { error } = await supabase.from(\'rc_lesson_progress\').upsert(\\n        {\\n          user_id: userId,\\n          lesson_id: lessonId,\\n          data,\\n          completed_at:\\n            typeof data[COMPLETED_KEY] === \'string\' ? data[COMPLETED_KEY] : null,\\n          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\\n        },\\n        { onConflict: \'user_id,lesson_id\' },\\n      );\\n      return !error;\\n    } catch {\\n      return false;\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  return {\\n    async load() {\\n      const local = await localAdapter.load();\\n      try {\\n        const { data, error } = await supabase\\n          .from(\'rc_lesson_progress\')\\n          .select(\'data, updated_at\')\\n          .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\n          .eq(\'lesson_id\', lessonId)\\n          .maybeSingle();\\n        if (error || !data || !isPlainRecord(data.data)) return local;\\n\\n        const latest = chooseLatestProgress(local, data.data as LessonData, data.updated_at);\\n        // Reconcile both stores on entry. This uploads work that was captured\\n        // locally during an outage and downloads newer work from another device.\\n        await localAdapter.save(latest);\\n        if (latest === local) cloudQueue = cloudQueue.then(() => writeCloud(latest));\\n        return latest;\\n      } catch {\\n        return local;\\n      }\\n    },\\n    async save(data) {\\n      const snapshot = stamp(data);\\n      let localOk = false;\\n      try {\\n        localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(snapshot));\\n        localOk = true;\\n      } catch {\\n        localOk = false;\\n      }\\n      cloudQueue = cloudQueue.then(() => writeCloud(snapshot));\\n      const cloudOk = await cloudQueue;\\n      return localOk && cloudOk;\\n    },\\n  };\\n}\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"c41d4c","wall_time_seconds":2.8514540999999998,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":4773,"output":"Warning: truncated output (original token count: 4773)\\nTotal output lines: 33\\n\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:106:      \\"Until about 1970, anyone who wanted to write a comprehensive history of medieval English law as it actually affected women would have found a dearth of published books or articles concerned with specific legal topics relating to women and derived from extensive research in actual court records. This is a serious deficiency, since court records are of vital importance in discovering how the law actually affected women, as opposed to how the law was intended to affect them or thought to affect them. These latter questions can be answered by consulting such sources as treatises, commentaries, and statutes; such texts were what most scholars of the nineteenth and early twentieth centuries concentrated on whenever they did write about medieval law. But these sources are of little help in determining, for example, how often women’s special statutory privileges were thwarted by intimidation or harassment, or how often women managed to evade special statutory limitations. And, quite apart from provisions designed to apply only, or especially, to women, they cannot tell us how general law affected the female half of the population—how women defendants and plaintiffs were treated in the courts in practice when they tried to exercise the rights they shared with men. Only quantitative studies of large numbers of cases would allow even a guess at the answers to these questions, and this scholarly work has been attempted by few.\\",\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:107:      \\"One can easily imagine why. Most medieval English court records are written in Latin or Anglo-Norman French and have never been published. The sheer volume of material to be sifted is daunting: there are over 27,500 parchment pages in the common plea rolls of the thirteenth century alone, every page nearly three feet long, and written often front and back in highly stylized court hand. But the difficulty of the sources, while it might appear to explain why the relevant scholarship has not been undertaken, seems actually to have deterred few: the fact is that few historians have wanted to write anything approaching women’s legal history in the first place. Most modern legal historians who have written on one aspect or another of special laws pertaining to women have begun with an interest in a legal idea or event or institution, not with a concern for how it affected women. Very few legal historians have started with an interest in women’s history that they might have elected to pursue through various areas of general law. And the result of all this is that the current state of our scholarly knowledge relating to law and the medieval Englishwoman is still fragmentary at best, though the situation is slowly improving.\\"\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:115:      \\"In contrast, some critics maintain that whatever authority judicial pronouncements have is exclusively institutional. Some of these critics go further, claiming that intellectual authority does not really exist—i.e., it reduces to institutional authority. But it can be countered that these claims break down when a sufficiently broad historical perspective is taken: Not all arguments accepted by institutions withstand the test of time, and some well-reasoned arguments never receive institutional imprimatur. The reasonable argument that goes unrecognized in its own time because it challenges institutional beliefs is common in intellectual history; intellectual authority and institutional consensus are not the same thing.\\",\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:152:      \\"In studying the autobiographies of Native Americans, most scholars have focused on as-told-to life histories that were solicited, translated, recorded, and edited by non-Native American collaborators—that emerged from “bicultural composite authorship.” Limiting their studies to such written documents, these scholars have overlooked traditional, preliterate modes of communicating personal history. In addition, they have failed to address the cultural constructs of the highly diverse Native American peoples, who prior to contact with nonindigenous cultures did not share with Europeans the same assumptions about self, life, and writing that underlie the concept of an autobiography—that indeed constitute the English word’s root meaning.\\",\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:153:      \\"The idea of self was, in a number of pre-contact Native American cultures, markedly inclusive: identity was not merely individual, but also relational to a society, a specific landscape, and the cosmos. Within these cultures, the expression of life experiences tended to be oriented toward current events: with the participation of fellow tribal members, an individual person would articulate, reenact, or record important experiences as the person lived them, a mode of autobiography seemingly more fragmented than the European custom of writing down the recollections of a lifetime. Moreover, expression itself was not a matter of writing but of language, which can include speech and signs. Oral autobiography comprised songs, chants, stories, and even the process whereby one repeatedly took on new names to reflect important events and deeds in one’s life. Dance and drama could convey personal history; for example, the advent of a vision to one person might require the enactment of that vision in the form of a tribal pageant.\\",\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:154:      \\"One can view as autobiographical the elaborate tattoos that symbolized a warrior’s valorous deeds, and such artifacts as a decorated shield that communicated the accomplishments and aspirations of its maker, or a robe that was emblazoned with the pictographic history of the wearer’s battles and was sometimes used in re-enactments. Also autobiographical, and indicative of high status within the tribe, would have been a tepee painted with symbolic designs to record the achievements and display the dreams or visions of its owner, who was often assisted in the painting by other tribal members.\\",\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:165:      \\"Thus there are misconceptions among humanists and scientists alike that are in need of correction. This correction leads to a much more acceptable position that could be called “scientific humanism,” attempting as it does to combine the common elements of both disciplines. Both science and the humanities attempt to describe and explain. It is true that they begin their descriptions and explanations at widely separated points, but the objectives remain the same: a clearer understanding of people and their world. In achieving this understanding, science in fact does not depend exclusively on measurable data, and the humanities in fact profit from attempts at controlled evaluation. Scientific humanism can combine the scientific attitude with an active interest in the whole scale of human values. If uninformed persons insist on viewing science as only materialistic and the humanities as only idealistic, a fruitful collaboration of both fields is unlikely. The combination of science and the humanities is, however, possible, even probable, if we begin by noting their common objectives, rather than seeing only their different means.\\"\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:199:      meta: [{ icon: \\"clock\\", text: \\"3.5 minutes\\" }, { icon: \\"book\\", text: \\"Two paragraphs\\" }, { icon: \\"pen\\", text: \\"Written response after\\" }],\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:224:      reveal: { buttonLabel: \\"See what usually happens\\", sectionLabel: \\"What usually happens\\", html: \\"<p>Students with strong reading skills can probably come up with a good response. But for the rest of us, it was probably quite a struggle. Chances are that you forgot as quickly as you read, there were names, keywords and bits and pieces of information that you remembered, but if I asked you to organize your thoughts into a coherent sentence or two, it became much mo…773 tokens truncated…ally exist—i.e., it reduces to institutional authority. But it can be countered that these claims break down when a sufficiently broad historical perspective is taken: Not all arguments accepted by institutions withstand the test of time, and some well-reasoned arguments never receive institutional imprimatur. The reasonable argument that goes unrecognized in its own time because it challenges institutional beliefs is common in intellectual history; intellectual authority and institutional consensus are not the same thing.</p>\\" },\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:393:      meta: [{ icon: \\"clock\\", text: \\"5 minutes\\" }, { icon: \\"book\\", text: \\"Three paragraphs\\" }, { icon: \\"pen\\", text: \\"Written response after\\" }],\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:487:      outro: \\"<p>So this passage, once you have simplified the concepts and grasped what at its core is a scientific debate, is fairly straightforward.</p>\\\\n    <p>Some scientists believe that the effects of volcanic eruptions on climate cooling are negligible.</p>\\\\n    <p>Other researchers think that even if the initial change is small, that can greatly impact climate cooling down the road.</p>\\\\n    <p>In this passage, the author explains the belief of some scientists and gives us the response of another group of scientists.</p>\\\\n    <p>We will go into more detail on how to tackle a passage that is full of abstract concepts and excruciating details down the road, but for now, just remember that if a passage contains such an explanation, try to simplify the concept that the author is trying to explain, and ask yourself why such an explanation is warranted?</p>\\",\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:513:        { kind: \\"quote\\", html: \\"<p>In studying the autobiographies of Native Americans, <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">most scholars have focused on as-told-to life histories that were solicited, translated, recorded, and edited by non-Native American collaborators</mark>—that emerged from “bicultural composite authorship.” Limiting their studies to such written documents, these scholars have <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">overlooked traditional, preliterate modes of communicating personal history</mark>. In addition, they have <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">failed to address the cultural constructs</mark> of the highly diverse Native American peoples, who prior to contact with nonindigenous cultures did not share with Europeans the same assumptions about self, life, and writing that underlie the concept of an autobiography—that indeed constitute the English word’s root meaning.</p>\\" },\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:515:        { kind: \\"quote\\", html: \\"<p>The idea of self was, in a number of pre-contact Native American cultures, markedly <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">inclusive</mark>: identity was not merely individual, but also relational to a society, a specific landscape, and the cosmos. Within these cultures, the expression of life experiences tended to be oriented toward current events: with the participation of fellow tribal members, an individual person would articulate, reenact, or record important experiences as the person lived them, a mode of autobiography seemingly more fragmented than the European custom of writing down the recollections of a lifetime. Moreover, expression itself was not a matter of writing but of language, which can include speech and signs. <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">Oral autobiography</mark> comprised songs, chants, stories, and even the process whereby one repeatedly took on new names to reflect important events and deeds in one’s life. <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">Dance and drama could convey personal history</mark>; for example, the advent of a vision to one person might require the enactment of that vision in the form of a tribal pageant.</p>\\" },\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:516:        { kind: \\"reaction\\", html: \\"<p>The author talks about how Native American cultural practices differ from European ones. The concept of an autobiography is alien as fellow tribe members can equally articulate one’s life experience. Oral history, dance, and ceremonies are all equally valid forms of re-enacting one’s life.</p>\\\\n      <p>Think about the differences between Native American and European practices here. The comparison framework works well.</p>\\" },\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:517:        { kind: \\"quote\\", html: \\"<p>One can view as autobiographical the elaborate <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">tattoos that symbolized a warrior’s valorous deeds</mark>, and such artifacts as a decorated <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">shield that communicated the accomplishments and aspirations of its maker</mark>, or a <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">robe that was emblazoned with the pictographic history of the wearer’s battles</mark> and was sometimes used in re-enactments. Also autobiographical, and indicative of high status within the tribe, would have been a <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">tepee painted with symbolic designs</mark> to record the achievements and display the dreams or visions of its owner, who was often assisted in the painting by other tribal members.</p>\\" },\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\content.ts:554:        { kind: \\"quote\\", html: \\"<p>Thus <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">there are misconceptions</mark> among humanists and scientists alike that are in need of correction. This correction leads to a much more acceptable position that could be called “<mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">scientific humanism</mark>,” attempting as it does to <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">combine the common elements of both disciplines</mark>. Both science and the humanities attempt to describe and explain. It is true that they begin their descriptions and explanations at widely separated points, but the <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">objectives remain the same</mark>: a clearer understanding of people and their world. In achieving this understanding, science in fact does not depend exclusively on measurable data, and the humanities in fact profit from attempts at controlled evaluation. Scientific humanism can <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">combine the scientific attitude with an active interest in the whole scale of human values</mark>. If uninformed persons insist on viewing science as only materialistic and the humanities as only idealistic, a fruitful collaboration of both fields is unlikely. The combination of science and the humanities is, however, possible, even probable, if we <mark class=\\\\\\"rc-mark\\\\\\">begin by noting their common objectives</mark>, rather than seeing only their different means.</p>\\" },\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\components\\\\CompletionScreen.tsx:4: * Plays back the student\'s own first from-memory attempt against the author\'s\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\components\\\\CompletionScreen.tsx:24:            <p>Here is your first attempt at the passage, written from memory after one timed read:</p>\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\components\\\\CompletionScreen.tsx:26:              <span className=\\"rc-quote-tag\\">Your first attempt</span>\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\components\\\\screens.tsx:87: * The student\'s own from-memory attempt, shown against the author\'s main point\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\components\\\\screens.tsx:94:  const attempt = String(state.data[FIRST_ATTEMPT_KEY] ?? \'\').trim();\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\components\\\\screens.tsx:95:  if (!attempt) return null;\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\components\\\\screens.tsx:99:      <p>{attempt}</p>\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\components\\\\screens.tsx:116:  // attempt belongs. Keyed off the callout, not a chapter number, so it cannot\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\components\\\\screens.tsx:273:    // navigating away cannot strand the last 600ms of a student\'s response.\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\components\\\\screens.tsx:300:        <div className=\\"rc-write-label\\">Your response</div>\\nsrc/bootcamps/rc-bootcamp\\\\lessonMeta.ts:11: * Storage key of the first from-memory write. 'text': '{"chunk_id":"ed6fb4","wall_time_seconds":0.6604528,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":416,"output":"/**\\n * Fire-and-forget logger for Intro to LR per-question attempts.\\n *\\n * Every answer the student submits in the bootcamp\'s InteractiveDrillRunner\\n * is recorded to `public.intro_lr_attempts`, scoped to the signed-in user.\\n * Errors are swallowed (with a console.warn) so a bad network never breaks\\n * the drill UI.\\n */\\n\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\n\\nexport interface IntroLrAttemptInput {\\n  drillId: string;\\n  questionId: string;\\n  mode: string;\\n  correct: boolean;\\n  selectedAnswer?: string | null;\\n  timeMs?: number | null;\\n}\\n\\n// In-memory de-dupe so re-renders or accidental double-fires don\'t double-log\\n// the same question response within a session.\\nconst seen = new Set<string>();\\n\\nexport function logIntroLrAttempt(input: IntroLrAttemptInput): void {\\n  void (async () => {\\n    try {\\n      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\\n      if (!user) return;\\n      const dedupeKey = `${user.id}::${input.drillId}::${input.questionId}::${input.correct}::${input.selectedAnswer ?? \'\'}`;\\n      if (seen.has(dedupeKey)) return;\\n      seen.add(dedupeKey);\\n\\n      const { error } = await supabase.from(\'intro_lr_attempts\').insert({\\n        user_id: user.id,\\n        drill_id: input.drillId,\\n        question_id: input.questionId,\\n        mode: input.mode,\\n        selected_answer: input.selectedAnswer ?? null,\\n        correct: input.correct,\\n        time_ms: input.timeMs ?? null,\\n      });\\n      if (error) {\\n        console.warn(\'[intro_lr_attempts] insert failed\', error.message);\\n        seen.delete(dedupeKey);\\n      }\\n    } catch (e) {\\n      console.warn(\'[intro_lr_attempts] log threw\', e);\\n    }\\n  })();\\n}\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"a4ed4c","wall_time_seconds":0.5066838,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":734,"output":"import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from \'react\';\\nimport { saveNativeLabSubmission, type NativeLabKind, type NativeLabSubmission } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/nativeLabAttempt\';\\n\\nexport function useNativeLabSave(kind?: NativeLabKind, userId?: string) {\\n  const run = useRef(crypto.randomUUID());\\n  const currentOwner = useRef(userId); currentOwner.current = userId;\\n  const mounted = useRef(true);\\n  useEffect(() => { mounted.current = true; return () => { mounted.current = false; }; }, []);\\n  const runOwner = useRef(userId);\\n  const ordinals = useRef(new Map<string, number>());\\n  const pending = useRef<NativeLabSubmission | null>(null);\\n  const busy = useRef(false);\\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\\n  async function save(questionId: string, answer: string, correct: boolean): Promise<boolean> {\\n    if (!kind) return true;\\n    if (busy.current) return false;\\n    busy.current = true; setSaving(true); setError(null);\\n    try {\\n      if (!userId) throw new Error(\'Sign in to save this lab answer.\');\\n      if (runOwner.current !== userId) {\\n        if (pending.current) throw new Error(\'A different account has an unconfirmed answer.\');\\n        runOwner.current = userId; run.current = crypto.randomUUID(); ordinals.current.clear();\\n      }\\n      if (!pending.current) pending.current = {\\n        id: crypto.randomUUID(), user_id: userId, drill_id: `native-lab:${kind}:${run.current}`,\\n        question_id: questionId, mode: \'native_lab_v1\', correct,\\n        selected_answer: JSON.stringify({ answer, ordinal: (ordinals.current.get(questionId) ?? 0) + 1, run_id: run.current }),\\n      };\\n      if (pending.current.user_id !== userId || pending.current.question_id !== questionId) throw new Error(\'Your pending answer belongs to a different account or question.\');\\n      const savedAnswer = JSON.parse(pending.current.selected_answer) as { answer: string };\\n      if (savedAnswer.answer !== answer || pending.current.correct !== correct) throw new Error(\'Retry the original pending answer before changing it.\');\\n      await saveNativeLabSubmission(pending.current);\\n      if (!mounted.current || currentOwner.current !== userId) return false;\\n      ordinals.current.set(questionId, (ordinals.current.get(questionId) ?? 0) + 1);\\n      pending.current = null;\\n      return true;\\n    } catch {\\n      if (mounted.current) setError(\'Your answer has not been confirmed in your account. Keep this page open and try saving again.\');\\n      return false;\\n    } finally { busy.current = false; if (mounted.current) setSaving(false); }\\n  }\\n  function restart() {\\n    if (busy.current || pending.current) return false;\\n    run.current = crypto.randomUUID(); ordinals.current.clear(); setError(null); return true;\\n  }\\n  return { save, restart, saving, error, blocked: saving || !!error, isCurrent: () => mounted.current && currentOwner.current === userId };\\n}\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"99e252","wall_time_seconds":2.3564567,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":246,"output":"src/pages/HardSentencesReviewDetail.tsx:5:import SavedReviewEpisodePanel from \'@/components/review/SavedReviewEpisodePanel\';\\nsrc/pages/HardSentencesReviewDetail.tsx:53:      {user?.id && <SavedReviewEpisodePanel ownerId={user.id} source={attempt.kind === \'cluster\' ? \'intro_lr_attempts\' : \'hs_capstone\'} sourceId={attempt.key} requestedEpisodeId={params.get(\'episode\')} />}\\nsrc/components/review/TeacherReviewSelections.tsx:41:        const selected = query.data.episodes.find(episode => episode.actor_id === scope.actorId && episode.reason === \'teacher_selected\' && episode.source_kind === binding?.source && episode.source_id === binding.sourceId);\\nsrc/pages/NativeLabReviewDetail.tsx:5:import SavedReviewEpisodePanel from \'@/components/review/SavedReviewEpisodePanel\';\\nsrc/pages/NativeLabReviewDetail.tsx:31:        {user?.id && attemptId && <SavedReviewEpisodePanel ownerId={user.id} source=\\"intro_lr_attempts\\" sourceId={attemptId} requestedEpisodeId={params.get(\'episode\')} />}\\n"}'}]

Your answer has not been saved.\');\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.from(\'intro_lr_attempts\').insert(submission).select(\'*\').single();\\n  if (!error && data && matches(data, submission)) return;\\n  // A response can be lost after the server inserts it. submission.user_id).eq(\'id\', submission.id).maybeSingle();\\n  if (!existing.error && existing.data && matches(existing.data, submission)) return;\\n  throw error ?? existing.error ?? new Error(\'The account did not acknowledge this saved answer.\');\\n}\\nfunction matches(row: Record<string, unknown>, expected: NativeLabSubmission): boolean {\\n  return default function TeacherReviewSelections({ studentId }: { studentId: string }) {\\n  const resolution = useStudentSubjectScope(studentId);\\n  if (resolution.state === \'loading\') return <p role=\\"status\\">Checking teacher review access…</p>;\\n  if (resolution.state !== \'ready\' || resolution.scope.mode !== \'selected_student\') return <p role=\\"status\\">Teacher selections require an authorized selected-student view.</p>;\\n  return <SelectionPanel key={`${resolution.scope.actorId}:${resolution.scope.studentId}`} scope={resolution.scope} />;\\n}\\nfunction SelectionPanel({ scope }: { scope: StudentSubjectScope }) {\\n  const query = useQuery({ queryKey: [\'review\', \'teacher-selections\', scope.actorId, scope.studentId], queryFn: () => loadScopedReviewSelections(scope), staleTime: 0 });\\n  const [chosen, setChosen] = useState<ReviewQueueItem | null>(null); const [reason, setReason] = useState(\'\');\\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false); const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false); const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);\\n  const mounted = useRef(true); const pending = useRef<{ item: ReviewQueueItem; reason: string } | null>(null);\\n  useEffect(() => { mounted.current = true; return () => { mounted.current = false; }; }, []);\\n  async function select() {\\n    if (!pending.current && chosen) pending.current = { item: chosen, reason };\\n    if (!pending.current) return;\\n    const request = pending.current; setBusy(true); setFailed(false); setSaved(false);\\n    try {\\n      await selectScopedReview(scope, request.item, request.reason);\\n      if (!mounted.current) return;\\n      pending.current = null; setSaved(true); void query.refetch();\\n    } catch { if (mounted.current) setFailed(true); }\\n    finally { if (mounted.current) setBusy(false); }\\n  }\\n  return <section aria-label=\\"Teacher-selected review\\" className=\\"space-y-3 border border-black/15 p-4\\">\\n    <h3 className=\\"font-semibold\\">Select a saved submission for review</h3>\\n    <p className=\\"text-xs text-black/60\\">This adds a review task with your reason to this student\'s Review queue. Supported here: saved misses from the three native Main Conclusion labs and Hard Sentences cluster/capstone. Original answers and flags stay unchanged.</p>\\n    {query.isLoading ? <p role=\\"status\\">Loading supported saved submissions…</p> : query.isError ? <div role=\\"alert\\"><p>{query.error instanceof StudentIdentityUnavailableError ? query.error.message : \'Supported review sources could not be loaded.\'}</p><button className=\\"min-h-11 underline\\" onClick={() => void query.refetch()}>Retry teacher review</button></div> : <>\\n      {!!query.data?.unavailable.length && <div role=\\"alert\\"><p>Partial review data: {query.data.unavailable.join(\', \')} could not be loaded.</p><button className=\\"min-h-11 underline\\" onClick={() => void query.refetch()}>Retry teacher review</button></div>}\\n      {!query.data?.items.length ? <p>No supported saved misses are available.</p> : <ul className=\\"space-y-2\\">{query.data.items.map(item => {\\n        const binding = boundedReviewBinding(item);\\n        const selected = query.data.episodes.find(episode => episode.actor_id === scope.actorId && episode.reason === \'teacher_selected\' && episode.source_kind === binding?.source && episode.source_id === binding.sourceId);\\n        const reviewed = selected && reviewEpisodeState(query.data.events.filter(event => event.episode_id === selected.id)).completed;\\n        return <li key={item.key} className=\\"border border-black/10 p-3\\"><strong className=\\"text-sm\\">{item.label}</strong>\\n          {selected ? <p className=\\"text-xs\\">Your saved reason: {selected.selection_reason} · {reviewed ? \'Reviewed by student\' : \'Waiting for student review\'}</p>\\n            : <button disabled={busy || pending.current !== null || !query.data.canSelect} className=\\"block min-h-11 text-xs underline\\" onClick={() => { setChosen(item); setReason(\'\'); setSaved(false); setFailed(false); }}>Choose this submission</button>}\\n        </li>;\\n      })}</ul>}\\n      {chosen && <div className=\\"space-y-2\\"><p className=\\"font-semibold text-sm\\">Selected: {chosen.label}</p>\\n        <label className=\\"block text-sm\\">Reason for the student<textarea aria-label=\\"Reason for the student\\" disabled={busy || pending.current !== null || saved} value={reason} onChange={event => setReason(event.target.value)} className=\\"block w-full border p-2\\" /></label>\\n        {saved ? <p role=\\"status\\">Teacher selection and reason are saved in the student\'s account.</p> : <button disabled={busy || !reason.trim() || !query.data?.canSelect} className=\\"min-h-11 underline\\" onClick={() => void select()}>{failed ? \'Retry teacher selection\' : \'Save teacher selection\'}</button>}\\n        {failed && <div role=\\"alert\\"><p>The selection was not confirmed. Your reason is retained. Retry this same selection, or reload to check for an existing saved reason.</p>\\n          <button disabled={busy} className=\\"min-h-11 underline\\" onClick={() => { pending.current = null; ## assistant

Review is substantially implemented. The main gap is extending saved review episodes beyond the narrow native-lab/Hard Sentences families, then proving those paths with actual owned records.

| Family | Implemented | Actual gaps |
|---|---|---|
| Native Intro to LR labs | Main Conclusion, First-Sentence vs Rebuttal, Conclusion Type save distinct submissions; queue and exact detail; student review episodes; teacher selection with reason. | Pending answer recovery survives retries while mounted, not reload. Broader live failure/reload/later-submission acceptance remains. Older native answers never saved cannot be recovered. |
| Historical Intro LR interactive | Seven authored drill families mapped to exact `intro_lr_attempts.id`; paginated missed-attempt queue; exact owner-filtered detail and authored reasoning. | Detail has no saved episode panel; `boundedReviewBinding` excludes its route; teacher selection loader excludes it. Producer uses fire-and-forget logging and in-memory semantic dedupe, so no durable launch/submission replay. |
| Hard Sentences cluster | Exact `intro_lr_attempts.id`, owner/source validation, queue/detail, saved episodes and teacher selection. | Cluster producer still uses fire-and-forget logger. First answer deliberately unknown; no reconstruction claim. |
| Hard Sentences capstone | Exact `bootcamp_kv.record_id` envelope, M5 payload validation, queue/detail, episodes and teacher selection. | Later UI/live acceptance still required. Historical envelope and payload UUIDs differ; preserve envelope identity. |
| Generic Activities | Completed assignment and timestamp required; attributable judged responses; exact assignment/item detail; first/latest answers; account/route stale-state guards; read retry. | No saved episode panel, completion projection or teacher selection binding. Positive actual owned Activity miss detail remains unverified; existing approved QA fixture was correct and does not establish missed-item Review. |
| RRC | Existing section history/report, owned recordings and grades elsewhere. | Explicitly excluded from generic Review by `mode === "section"` / `item_format === "spoken-recall"`. No RRC queue adapter, exact central Review route or episodes. Requires a specific criterion for review eligibility; absence of judgment is not an incorrect answer. |
| Other bootcamps | Official bank questions may enter existing journal/section routes; three native labs are supported. | No general authored exercise adapter or common immutable submission contract. Queue expressly discloses other bootcamp exercises excluded. RC lesson progress is a mutable snapshot, not an attempt ledger. |
| Teacher-selected | Durable admin reason, canonical selected-student resolution, actor checks, exact supported submission, student completion state. | UI offers only saved misses from native labs and Hard Sentences. Historical Intro LR, Activities, RRC and other bootcamp sources are absent. Teacher feedback events and selection of correct/flagged/skipped work are not implemented broadly. |

The exact identity contracts matter:

- Native: `intro_lr_attempts.id` per response; `drill_id = native-lab:<kind>:<run UUID>`; JSON answer includes `run_id` and per-question ordinal. First-answer label only for ordinal 1. Retry reconciles the same response UUID.
- Historical/cluster: database-generated attempt ID; current logger dedupes by actor/drill/question/correct/answer within memory. This is not durable distinct-retake identity.
- HS capstone: owner + `table_name=calibration_attempts` + envelope `record_id`, payload M5/item ID.
- Activity: assignment UUID + item order identifies the sitting item; append-only response UUID and attempt number identify its submissions. Current queue aggregates that item's judged attempts.
- RRC: assignment UUID + attempt number + source section; stimulus identity and per-stimulus saved response/grade must be retained for a new adapter.
- Episodes: unique `(student_id, source_kind, source_id, reason, actor_id)`; separate student miss and teacher selection. New source attempt means new episode; completing one never suppresses another.

Backend episodes already exist and were deployed. `docs/agent/verification/2026-09-30-review-episodes.json` and `…-live.json` record deployed SQL, 26 ordinary-JWT checks, student/admin agreement and unchanged source digest. The SQL artifact's “unapplied” header and client comment are stale. No fresh backend inspection was performed in this audit.

The contract enables owner/admin SELECT with RLS and denies direct application writes; protected RPCs resolve source ownership. Only the student can append review work. Event UUID retries return exact prior payload; blind answer is immutable before reveal; completion is terminal and a missed completion requires two diagnoses. Supported source enums remain only `intro_lr_attempts` and `hs_capstone`. Source tables have separate permissions: the original `intro_lr_attempts` migration permits student-owned management, so ownership verification should not be described as independently certified historical correctness.

Evidence files and code:

- `src/lib/scopedReviewEpisodes.ts:10` restricts binding to native and Hard Sentences routes.
- `src/lib/reviewEpisodes.ts:4` restricts source enums; SQL artifact mirrors them.
- `src/pages/IntroLrReviewDetail.tsx` has no episode panel.
- `src/components/review/reviewQueueModel.ts:107–152` gates/aggregates Activities and excludes RRC.
- `src/components/review/TeacherReviewSelections.tsx:34` discloses its narrow supported families.
- `src/components/review/SavedReviewEpisodePanel.tsx` retains pending event in a ref and warns source feedback may already be visible. Recording Reveal here is not historical Blind Review.
- `docs/implementation-plans/2026-10-02-review-170-integrity-verification.md` records 87 tests, actual responsive/read-failure checks and publication `cc294c7`; its positive runtime check was journal filter/detail/return, not every source family's completion.

Most efficient execution:

1. Finish acceptance of existing native/HS episodes using owned exact sources: answer, reveal, reflection, completion, reload, queue suppression, teacher agreement, later miss, source/flag preservation.
2. Add historical Intro LR episode binding/panel and teacher source loading. Existing backend already resolves these exact `intro_lr_attempts` IDs; avoid a replacement subsystem. Address logging durability separately for future submissions.
3. Extend the existing episode contract for Activity response identity and exact assignment/item detail. Use additive protected source resolution and reuse panel/projection/teacher controls.
4. Implement RRC as a separate adapter against its grade/report semantics, preserving assignments, attempts and stimulus IDs. Keep unjudged recordings explicit.
5. Inventory remaining bootcamp producers and adopt a common immutable submission contract only where missing. Never fabricate historical submissions from mutable progress.

No edits or tests rerun.
